full text
The M1 build has stopped and needs your decisions. 24 of 30 tasks have landed on `build/M1` and nothing is running. Four tasks are blocked; M1-T23 waits on them, and the final task M1-T30 waits on that.

**1. M1-T13, baseline means (contract change).** The agent couldn't create `SnakeRule` because the frozen class had no `description` field, so the type checker refuses to create one. The agent's own suggested fix is to run it after M1-T15, which has since landed and may supply that field.
- *My recommendation:* a plain retry, with no plan change.

**2. M1-T26, tests for the verdict rule (contract change).** The contract has no standalone verdict function, so a tester can't feed in the test case's numbers without running the whole final evaluation.
- *Options:* add a pure `evaluate.verdict(...)` function to the contract, or change M1-T26 to a single-agent task where one agent writes both the tests and the code.
- *My recommendation:* the single-agent change, because it's smaller.

**3. M1-T28, mixed-settings chart refusal (contract change).** The check itself is written, but the task may only edit `charts/check.py`. Hooking the check into the chart needs a change in `charts/curve.py`.
- *Fix:* allow the task to edit `curve.py` too.

**4. M1-T29, chart style.** The agent says one test is wrong: it reads the chart title from the centre position, but the chart sets it on the left. It also needs `curve.py` to apply the style.
- *Options:* rewrite the test with a fresh tester, or keep the tests and allow the task to edit `curve.py`.
- *My recommendation:* keep the tests and allow `curve.py`. The real gap is which files the task may edit, not the test. M1-T28 and M1-T29 would then both edit `curve.py`, so the replan has to run one after the other.

**The 8 Important findings** from the interface review are also waiting. The same replan can turn them into fix tasks.

**Next steps if you take my recommendations:**
1. Record a retry for M1-T13, and record "tests stand" for M1-T29. Each goes through `dt build resolve` with your words as the note.
2. Run a `build-planning` replan: change M1-T26 to a single-agent task, let M1-T28 and M1-T29 edit `curve.py` one after the other, and add fix tasks for the findings. You approve the replan.
3. You give a new go, and the build continues to the phase review, then M1-T30 and the exit review.

The details for each blocked task are in `.git/dt-build/details/` (`M1-T13.solo-1.txt`, `M1-T26.tester-1.txt`, `M1-T28.implementer-1.txt`, `M1-T29.implementer-1.txt`).

Which of these do you want?
